fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Run and manage Jobs</span></h1><!--]--><!----> <p>The Hugging Face Hub provides compute for AI and data workflows via Jobs.</p> <p><strong>For a general overview of Jobs and pricing, see the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/hub/jobs" rel="nofollow">Hub Jobs documentation</a>.</strong></p> <p>A job runs on Hugging Face infrastructure and are defined with a command to run (e.g. a python command), a Docker Image from Hugging Face Spaces or Docker Hub, and a hardware flavor (CPU, GPU, TPU). This guide will show you how to interact with Jobs on the Hub, especially:</p> <ul><li>Run a job.</li> <li>Check job status.</li> <li>Select the hardware.</li> <li>Configure environment variables and secrets.</li> <li>Run UV scripts.</li></ul> <p>If you want to run and manage a job on the Hub, your machine must be logged in. If you are not, please refer to <a href="../quick-start#authentication">this section</a>. In the rest of this guide, we will assume that your machine is logged in.</p> <blockquote class="tip"><p><strong>Hugging Face Jobs</strong> are available to any user or organization with a positive <a href="https://huggingface.co/settings/billing" rel="nofollow">credit balance</a>. See <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/hub/jobs-pricing" rel="nofollow">Jobs pricing and billing</a> for details.</p></blockquote> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="jobs-command-line-interface" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#jobs-command-line-interface"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Jobs Command Line Interface</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Use the <a href="./cli#hf-jobs"><code>hf jobs</code> CLI</a> to run Jobs from the command line, and pass <code>--flavor</code> to specify your hardware.</p> <p><code>hf jobs run</code> runs Jobs with a Docker image and a command with a familiar Docker-like interface. Think <code>docker run</code>, but for running code on any hardware:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --name hello-world python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Hello world&#x27;)&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --name gpu-check --flavor a10g-small pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel python -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import torch; print(torch.cuda.get_device_name())&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use <code>hf jobs uv run</code> to run local or remote UV scripts:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --name my-script my_script.py
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --name sft-training --flavor a10g-small <span class="hljs-string">&quot;https://raw.githubusercontent.com/huggingface/trl/main/trl/scripts/sft.py&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>UV scripts are Python scripts that include their dependencies directly in the file using a special comment syntax defined in the <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/guides/scripts/" rel="nofollow">UV documentation</a>.</p> <p>Now the rest of this guide will show you the python API.
If you would like to view all the available <code>hf jobs</code> commands and options instead, check out the <a href="./cli#hf-jobs">guide on the <code>hf jobs</code> command line interface</a>.</p> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Need an <em>interactive</em> machine instead of a fire-and-forget job — e.g. to run AI-generated code, with command execution and file transfer? Check out <a href="./sandbox">Sandboxes</a>, built on top of Jobs.</p></blockquote> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="run-a-job" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#run-a-job"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Run a Job</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Run compute Jobs defined with a command and a Docker Image on Hugging Face infrastructure (including GPUs and TPUs).</p> <p>You can only manage Jobs that you own (under your username namespace) or from organizations in which you have write permissions.
This feature is pay-as-you-go: you only pay for the seconds you use.</p> <p><a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_job">run_job()</a> lets you run any command on Hugging Face’s infrastructure:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Directly run Python code</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Hello from the cloud!&#x27;)&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Use GPUs without any setup</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import torch; print(torch.cuda.get_device_name())&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-small&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run in an organization account</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Running in an org account&#x27;)&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    namespace=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;my-org-name&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run from Hugging Face Spaces</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;hf.co/spaces/lhoestq/duckdb&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;duckdb&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;select &#x27;hello world&#x27;&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run a Python script with `uv` (experimental)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;my_script.py&quot;</span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="warning"><p><strong>Important</strong>: Jobs have a default timeout (30 minutes), after which they will automatically stop. For long-running tasks like model training, make sure to set a custom timeout using the <code>timeout</code> parameter. See <a href="#configure-job-timeout">Configure Job Timeout</a> for details.</p></blockquote> <p><a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_job">run_job()</a> returns the <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/jobs#huggingface_hub.JobInfo">JobInfo</a> which has the URL of the Job on Hugging Face, where you can see the Job status and the logs.
Save the Job ID from <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/jobs#huggingface_hub.JobInfo">JobInfo</a> to manage the job:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Hello from the cloud!&#x27;)&quot;</span>]
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job.url
https://huggingface.co/jobs/lhoestq/687f911eaea852de79c4a50a
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>
687f911eaea852de79c4a50a<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Jobs run in the background. The next section guides you through <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.inspect_job">inspect_job()</a> to know a jobs’ status, <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.fetch_job_logs">fetch_job_logs()</a> to view the logs and <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.fetch_job_metrics">fetch_job_metrics()</a> to monitor resources usage.</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative List your jobs (results are paginated and returned as an iterator)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> list_jobs
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>jobs = list_jobs()

JobInfo(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;687f911eaea852de79c4a50a&#x27;</span>, created_at=datetime.datetime(<span class="hljs-number">2025</span>, <span class="hljs-number">7</span>, <span class="hljs-number">22</span>, <span class="hljs-number">13</span>, <span class="hljs-number">24</span>, <span class="hljs-number">46</span>, <span class="hljs-number">909000</span>, tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc), docker_image=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;python:3.12&#x27;</span>, space_id=<span class="hljs-literal">None</span>, command=[<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;python&#x27;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;-c&#x27;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Hello from the cloud!&#x27;)&quot;</span>], arguments=[], environment={}, secrets={}, flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;cpu-basic&#x27;</span>, status=JobStatus(stage=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;COMPLETED&#x27;</span>, message=<span class="hljs-literal">None</span>), owner=JobOwner(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;5e9ecfc04957053f60648a3e&#x27;</span>, name=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;lhoestq&#x27;</span>), endpoint=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;https://huggingface.co&#x27;</span>, url=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;https://huggingface.co/jobs/lhoestq/687f911eaea852de79c4a50a&#x27;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Materialize the iterator into a list</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>all_jobs = [job <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> job <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> list_jobs()]

<span class="hljs-comment"># List your running jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>running_jobs = list_jobs(status=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;RUNNING&quot;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Filter by one or more statuses and/or labels</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>list_jobs(status=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;RUNNING&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;SCHEDULING&quot;</span>], labels={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;env&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;prod&quot;</span>})

<span class="hljs-comment"># Inspect the status of a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> inspect_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>inspect_job(job_id=job_id)
JobInfo(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;687f911eaea852de79c4a50a&#x27;</span>, created_at=datetime.datetime(<span class="hljs-number">2025</span>, <span class="hljs-number">7</span>, <span class="hljs-number">22</span>, <span class="hljs-number">13</span>, <span class="hljs-number">24</span>, <span class="hljs-number">46</span>, <span class="hljs-number">909000</span>, tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc), docker_image=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;python:3.12&#x27;</span>, space_id=<span class="hljs-literal">None</span>, command=[<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;python&#x27;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;-c&#x27;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;Hello from the cloud!&#x27;)&quot;</span>], arguments=[], environment={}, secrets={}, flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;cpu-basic&#x27;</span>, status=JobStatus(stage=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;COMPLETED&#x27;</span>, message=<span class="hljs-literal">None</span>), owner=JobOwner(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;5e9ecfc04957053f60648a3e&#x27;</span>, name=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;lhoestq&#x27;</span>), endpoint=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;https://huggingface.co&#x27;</span>, url=<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;https://huggingface.co/jobs/lhoestq/687f911eaea852de79c4a50a&#x27;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># View logs from a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> fetch_job_logs
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> log <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> fetch_job_logs(job_id=job_id):
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(log)
Hello <span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> the cloud!

<span class="hljs-comment"># View resources usage metrics from a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> fetch_job_metrics
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> metrics <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> fetch_job_metrics(job_id=job_id):
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(metrics)
{
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;cpu_usage_pct&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">0</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;cpu_millicores&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">2000</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;memory_used_bytes&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">929792</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;memory_total_bytes&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">17179869184</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;rx_bps&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">0</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;tx_bps&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-number">0</span>,
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;gpus&quot;</span>: {},
    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;replica&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;4dzsh&quot;</span>
}

<span class="hljs-comment"># Cancel a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> cancel_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>cancel_job(job_id=job_id)<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="wait-until-jobs-finish" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#wait-until-jobs-finish"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Wait until Jobs finish</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Use <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.wait_for_job">wait_for_job()</a> to block until a Job reaches a terminal stage (<code>COMPLETED</code>, <code>CANCELED</code>, <code>ERROR</code> or <code>DELETED</code>). The final <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/jobs#huggingface_hub.JobInfo">JobInfo</a> is always returned — a failed Job does not raise an exception — so check <code>job.status.stage</code> to act on the outcome. Pass a list of Job IDs to wait on a whole batch at once.</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job, wait_for_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>, command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;hello&#x27;)&quot;</span>])
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>wait_for_job(job_id=job.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>).status.stage
<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;COMPLETED&#x27;</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run multiple jobs in parallel and wait for all of them to finish</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>jobs = [run_job(image=image, command=command) <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> command <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> commands]
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>finished_jobs = wait_for_job(job_id=[job.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> job <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> jobs], timeout=<span class="hljs-number">3600</span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The same is available in the CLI with <code>hf jobs wait</code>, which exits with code 0 only if all Jobs completed successfully — handy for chaining commands in shell scripts or CI:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Chain on success</span>
hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">wait</span> &lt;job_id&gt; &amp;&amp; hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --detach python:3.12 python eval.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Wait for all currently running jobs</span>
hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -q | xargs hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">wait</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Note that a non-detached <code>hf jobs run</code> (or <code>hf jobs uv run</code>) also exits with a non-zero code if the Job fails, so <code>hf jobs run ... &amp;&amp; next-step</code> chains correctly without an explicit wait.</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="select-the-hardware" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#select-the-hardware"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Select the hardware</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>There are numerous cases where running Jobs on GPUs are useful:</p> <ul><li><strong>Model Training</strong>: Fine-tune or train models on GPUs (T4, A10G, A100) without managing infrastructure</li> <li><strong>Synthetic Data Generation</strong>: Generate large-scale datasets using LLMs on powerful hardware</li> <li><strong>Data Processing</strong>: Process massive datasets with high-CPU configurations for parallel workloads</li> <li><strong>Batch Inference</strong>: Run offline inference on thousands of samples using optimized GPU setups</li> <li><strong>Experiments &amp; Benchmarks</strong>: Run ML experiments on consistent hardware for reproducible results</li> <li><strong>Development &amp; Debugging</strong>: Test GPU code without local CUDA setup</li></ul> <p>Run jobs on GPUs or TPUs with the <code>flavor</code> argument. For example, to run a PyTorch job on an A10G GPU:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Use an A10G GPU to check PyTorch CUDA</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import torch; print(f&#x27;This code ran with the following GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name()}&#x27;)&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-small&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Running this will show the following output!</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!---->This code ran with the following GPU: NVIDIA A10G<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use this to run a fine-tuning script like <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/trl/blob/main/trl/scripts/sft.py" rel="nofollow">trl/scripts/sft.py</a> with UV:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;sft.py&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    script_args=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;--model_name_or_path&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;Qwen/Qwen2-0.5B&quot;</span>, ...],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    dependencies=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;trl&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    env={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;HF_TOKEN&quot;</span>: ...},
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-small&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="tip"><p>For comprehensive guidance on running model training jobs with TRL on Hugging Face infrastructure, check out the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/trl/main/en/jobs_training" rel="nofollow">TRL Jobs Training documentation</a>. It covers fine-tuning recipes, hardware selection, and best practices for training models efficiently.</p></blockquote> <p>Here is the full list of available hardware to run Jobs:</p> GB)</td><td>$0.3917</td><td>$23.50</td></tr></tbody></table> <p>You can get this list programmatically by running:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> hardware<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Or using the Python API:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> list_jobs_hardware
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>list_jobs_hardware()<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>That’s it! You’re now running code on Hugging Face’s infrastructure.</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="mount-a-volume" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#mount-a-volume"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Mount a volume</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Mount a volume on the Jobs’s disk using a list of <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/jobs#huggingface_hub.Volume">Volume</a>.</p> <p>You can mount any Hugging Face Repository (model/dataset/space) or <a href="/docs/hub/storage-buckets">Storage Bucket</a>. For example:</p> <ul><li>mount a model repository: <code>Volume(type="model", source="openai/gpt-oss-120b", mount_path="/model")</code></li> <li>mount a dataset repository: <code>Volume(type="dataset", source="HuggingFaceFW/fineweb", mount_path="/data")</code></li> <li>mount a storage bucket: <code>Volume(type="bucket", source="username/my-bucket", mount_path="/mnt")</code></li></ul> <p>Then you can use the mounted volume as a local directory:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job, Volume
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;duckdb/duckdb&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;duckdb&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;SELECT * FROM &#x27;/data/**/*.parquet&#x27; LIMIT 5&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    volumes=[Volume(<span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;dataset&quot;</span>, source=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;HuggingFaceFW/fineweb&quot;</span>, mount_path=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;/data&quot;</span>)],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>You can also write to a mounted bucket, for example, to save checkpoints when training a model:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job, Volume
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>script = <span class="hljs-string">&quot;my_sft.py&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>script_args = [<span class="hljs-string">&quot;--output_dir&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;/training-outputs/training-v3-final&quot;</span>, ...]
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>checkpoints_bucket = Volume(<span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;bucket&quot;</span>, source=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;username/my-bucket&quot;</span>, mount_path=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;/training-outputs&quot;</span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(script, script_args=script_args, volumes=[checkpoints_bucket])<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>By default, mounted storage buckets have read+write abilities.
This is especially useful for storage buckets, which provide fast, mutable storage for data that changes frequently — files can be overwritten or deleted in place.</p> <p>Use <code>read_only=True</code> to enable read-only: <code>Volume(type="bucket", read_only=True, ...)</code>.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="mount-local-data" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#mount-local-data"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Mount local data</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>To run a Job against data on your machine, use <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.sync_job_volume">sync_job_volume()</a>: it syncs a local directory to your <code>jobs-artifacts</code> <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/hub/storage-buckets" rel="nofollow">Storage Bucket</a> (created automatically if needed) and returns a ready-to-mount <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/jobs#huggingface_hub.Volume">Volume</a>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job, sync_job_volume

<span class="hljs-comment"># Upload ./training-data once...</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>volume = sync_job_volume(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;./training-data&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;/data&quot;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># ...then run as many Jobs as you want against it</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;train.py&quot;</span>, script_args=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;--learning-rate&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;0.01&quot;</span>], volumes=[volume])
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;train.py&quot;</span>, script_args=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;--learning-rate&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;0.05&quot;</span>], volumes=[volume])<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Each directory gets its own stable folder in the bucket: re-running <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.sync_job_volume">sync_job_volume()</a> on the same directory only uploads new or modified files. The volume is mounted read-only by default.</p> <p>To retrieve files written by a Job, mount a read-write volume (an empty output directory works too) and sync it back once the Job is over:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job, sync_bucket, sync_job_volume

<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>outputs = sync_job_volume(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;./outputs&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;/outputs&quot;</span>, read_only=<span class="hljs-literal">False</span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;process.py&quot;</span>, volumes=[outputs])

<span class="hljs-comment"># ...once the Job completes, pull back the data:</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>sync_bucket(<span class="hljs-string">f&quot;hf://buckets/<span class="hljs-subst">{outputs.source}</span>/<span class="hljs-subst">{outputs.path}</span>&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;./outputs&quot;</span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>In the CLI, simply pass a local directory as the source side of <code>-v</code>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run -v ./pdfs:/input -v ./md-out:/output:rw ocr.py<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="ssh-into-a-job" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#ssh-into-a-job"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>SSH into a Job</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Pass <code>ssh=True</code> to <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_job">run_job()</a> (or <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_uv_job">run_uv_job()</a>) to make the Job’s container reachable over SSH. The SSH endpoint is available in the Job status:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;sleep&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;infinity&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    ssh=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job.status.ssh_url
<span class="hljs-string">&#x27;ssh://68498e23210b3a4f4e6e2a23@ssh.hf.jobs&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Connect from a terminal with <code>hf jobs ssh &lt;job_id></code> (or directly with <code>ssh &lt;job_id>@ssh.hf.jobs</code>):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ssh 68498e23210b3a4f4e6e2a23<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Only users with write access to the Job’s namespace are allowed in (the Job creator, or members of the owner organization), authenticated by an SSH public key registered at <a href="https://huggingface.co/settings/keys" rel="nofollow">https://huggingface.co/settings/keys</a>.</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="configure-job-timeout" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#configure-job-timeout"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Configure Job Timeout</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Jobs have a default timeout (30 minutes), after which they will automatically stop. This is important to know when running long-running tasks like model training.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="setting-a-custom-timeout" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#setting-a-custom-timeout"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Setting a custom timeout</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>You can specify a custom timeout value using the <code>timeout</code> parameter when running a job. The timeout can be specified in two ways:</p> <ol><li><strong>As a number</strong> (interpreted as seconds):</li></ol> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;train_model.py&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-large&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    timeout=<span class="hljs-number">7200</span>,  <span class="hljs-comment"># 2 hours in seconds</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <ol start="2"><li><strong>As a string with time units</strong>:</li></ol> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># Using different time units</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;train_model.py&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-large&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    timeout=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;2h&quot;</span>,  <span class="hljs-comment"># 2 hours</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># Other examples:</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># timeout=&quot;30m&quot;    # 30 minutes</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># timeout=&quot;1.5h&quot;   # 1.5 hours</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># timeout=&quot;1d&quot;     # 1 day</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># timeout=&quot;3600s&quot;  # 3600 seconds</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Supported time units:</p> <ul><li><code>s</code> - seconds</li> <li><code>m</code> - minutes</li> <li><code>h</code> - hours</li> <li><code>d</code> - days</li></ul> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="using-timeout-with-uv-jobs" class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = run_uv_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-string">&quot;training_script.py&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-large&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    timeout=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;90m&quot;</span>,  <span class="hljs-comment"># 90 minutes</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="warning"><p>If you don’t specify a timeout, a default timeout will be applied to your job. For long-running tasks like model training that may take hours, make sure to set an appropriate timeout to avoid unexpected job terminations.</p></blockquote> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="monitoring-job-duration" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Monitoring job duration</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>When running long tasks, it’s good practice to:</p> <ul><li>Estimate your job’s expected duration and set a timeout with some buffer</li> <li>Monitor your job’s progress through the logs</li> <li>Check the job status to ensure it hasn’t timed out</li></ul> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> inspect_job, fetch_job_logs
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-comment"># Check job status</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job_info = inspect_job(job_id=job.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> job_info.status.stage == <span class="hljs-string">&quot;ERROR&quot;</span>:
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(<span class="hljs-string">f&quot;Job failed: <span class="hljs-subst">{job_info.status.message}</span>&quot;</span>)
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-comment"># Check logs for more details</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> log <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> fetch_job_logs(job_id=job.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>):
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>        <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(log)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>For more details about the timeout parameter, see the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/huggingface_hub/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_job.timeout" rel="nofollow"><code>run_job</code> API reference</a>.</p> <!--[1--><h2 relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z"
