rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass environment variables</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import os; print(os.environ[&#x27;FOO&#x27;], os.environ[&#x27;BAR&#x27;])&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    env={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;FOO&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;foo&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;BAR&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;bar&quot;</span>},
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass secrets - they will be encrypted server side</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import os; print(os.environ[&#x27;MY_SECRET&#x27;])&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    secrets={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;MY_SECRET&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;psswrd&quot;</span>},
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="built-in-environment-variables" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#built-in-environment-variables"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Built-in Environment Variables</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Inside the job container, the following environment variables are automatically available:</p> <table><thead><tr><th>Variable</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>JOB_ID</code></td><td>The unique identifier of the current job. Use this to reference the job programmatically, for example to store outputs in a dataset with a unique name.</td></tr><tr><td><code>ACCELERATOR</code></td><td>The type of accelerator available (e.g., <code>t4-medium</code>, <code>a10g-small</code>, <code>a100x4</code>). Empty if no accelerator.</td></tr><tr><td><code>CPU_CORES</code></td><td>The number of CPU cores available to the job (e.g., <code>2</code>, <code>4</code>, <code>8</code>).</td></tr><tr><td><code>MEMORY</code></td><td>The amount of memory available to the job (e.g., <code>16Gi</code>, <code>32Gi</code>).</td></tr></tbody></table> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Access job environment information</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;&quot;&quot;
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>import os
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>print(f&quot;Job ID: {os.environ.get(&#x27;JOB_ID&#x27;)}&quot;)
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>print(f&quot;Accelerator: {os.environ.get(&#x27;ACCELERATOR&#x27;, &#x27;none&#x27;)}&quot;)
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>print(f&quot;CPU cores: {os.environ.get(&#x27;CPU_CORES&#x27;)}&quot;)
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>print(f&quot;Memory: {os.environ.get(&#x27;MEMORY&#x27;)}&quot;)
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>&quot;&quot;&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>These variables are useful when you need to create unique identifiers for outputs, adapt your code based on available hardware, or log resource information.</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="labels" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#labels"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Labels</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Labels are key=value pairs that attach metadata to a Job:</p> <p>Use <code>name</code> to make Jobs easier to find and identify in the UI. Names are optional and do not have to be unique:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(name=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;daily-report&quot;</span>, image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>, command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;report.py&quot;</span>])<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>From the CLI, pass <code>--name</code> when creating a Job, or name an existing Job through the labels command:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --name daily-report python:3.12 python report.py
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> labels &lt;job_id&gt; --name daily-report<!----></pre></div><!----> <p><code>hf jobs ls</code> shows a <code>NAME</code> column and can filter by name (a shortcut for <code>--label name=NAME</code>):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -a --name daily-report<!----></pre></div><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass extra metadata with Labels</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import os; print(os.environ[&#x27;MY_SECRET&#x27;])&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    labels={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;my-label&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;my-value&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;foo&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;bar&quot;</span>},
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>If you don’t pass <code>--name</code>, a name is derived automatically from the Docker image or the script, plus a short hash of the command so reruns of the same command share a name (e.g. <code>python:3.12 foo --truc</code> → <code>python-3-12-1a2b3c4d</code>).</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="update-labels" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#update-labels"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Update labels</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Use <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.update_job_labels">update_job_labels()</a> to replace labels on an existing job. This replaces all existing user-provided labels:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> update_job_labels
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>update_job_labels(job_id, labels={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;env&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;prod&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;team&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;ml&quot;</span>})<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>This also works for scheduled jobs with <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.update_scheduled_job_labels">update_scheduled_job_labels()</a>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> update_scheduled_job_labels
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>update_scheduled_job_labels(scheduled_job_id, labels={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;env&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;staging&quot;</span>})<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>From the CLI:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> labels &lt;job_id&gt; --label <span class="hljs-built_in">env</span>=prod --label team=ml
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled labels &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt; --label <span class="hljs-built_in">env</span>=staging<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>To remove all labels, pass <code>--clear</code>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> labels &lt;job_id&gt; --clear<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="uv-scripts-experimental" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#uv-scripts-experimental"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>UV Scripts (Experimental)</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Looking for ready-to-use UV scripts? Check out the <a href="https://huggingface.co/uv-scripts" rel="nofollow">uv-scripts organization</a> on the Hugging Face Hub, which offers a community collection of UV scripts for tasks like model training, synthetic data generation, data processing, and more.</p></blockquote> <p>Run UV scripts (Python scripts with inline dependencies) on HF infrastructure:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Run a UV script (creates temporary repo)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> run_uv_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;my_script.py&quot;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run with GPU</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;ml_training.py&quot;</span>, flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;gpu-t4-small&quot;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run with dependencies</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;inference.py&quot;</span>, dependencies=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;transformers&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;torch&quot;</span>])

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run a script directly from a URL</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;https://huggingface.co/datasets/username/scripts/resolve/main/example.py&quot;</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run a command</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>run_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>, script_args=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import lighteval&quot;</span>], dependencies=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;lighteval&quot;</span>])<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>UV scripts are Python scripts that include their dependencies directly in the file using a special comment syntax. This makes them perfect for self-contained tasks that don’t require complex project setups. Learn more about UV scripts in the <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/guides/scripts/" rel="nofollow">UV documentation</a>.</p> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="docker-images-for-uv-scripts" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#docker-images-for-uv-scripts"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Docker Images for UV Scripts</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p>While UV scripts can specify their dependencies inline, ML workloads often have complex dependencies. Using pre-built Docker images with these libraries already installed can significantly speed up job startup and avoid dependency issues.</p> <p>By default, when you run <code>hf jobs uv run</code> the <code>astral-sh/uv:python3.12-bookworm</code> image is used. This image is based on the Python 3.12 Bookworm distribution with uv pre-installed.</p> <p>You can specify a different image using the <code>--image</code> flag:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!---->hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run \
 --flavor a10g-large \
 --image vllm/vllm-openai:latest \
...<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The above command will run using the <code>vllm/vllm-openai:latest</code> image. This approach could be useful if you are using vLLM for synthetic data generation.</p> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Many inference frameworks provide optimized docker images. As uv is increasingly adopted in the Python ecosystem more of these will also have uv pre-installed meaning they will work when using hf jobs uv run.</p></blockquote> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Scheduled Jobs</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Schedule and manage jobs that will run on HF infrastructure.</p> <p>Use <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.create_scheduled_job">create_scheduled_job()</a> or <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.create_scheduled_uv_job">create_scheduled_uv_job()</a> with a schedule of <code>@annually</code>, <code>@yearly</code>, <code>@monthly</code>, <code>@weekly</code>, <code>@daily</code>, <code>@hourly</code>, or a CRON schedule expression (e.g., <code>"0 9 * * 1"</code> for 9 AM every Monday):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule a job that runs every hour</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> create_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>create_scheduled_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>,  <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;This runs every hour!&#x27;)&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    schedule=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;@hourly&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Use the CRON syntax</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>create_scheduled_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python:3.12&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>,  <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;This runs every 5 minutes!&#x27;)&quot;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    schedule=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;*/5 * * * *&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule with GPU</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>create_scheduled_job(
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    image=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    command=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;python&quot;</span>,  <span class="hljs-string">&quot;-c&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import torch; print(f&quot;This code ran with the following GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name()}&quot;)&#x27;</span>],
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    schedule=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;@hourly&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    flavor=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;a10g-small&quot;</span>,
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule a UV script</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> create_scheduled_uv_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>create_scheduled_uv_job(<span class="hljs-string">&quot;my_script.py&quot;</span>, schedule=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;@hourly&quot;</span>)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use the same parameters as <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_job">run_job()</a> and <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.run_uv_job">run_uv_job()</a> to pass environment variables, secrets, timeout, etc.</p> <p>Manage scheduled jobs using <code>list_scheduled_jobs</code>, <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.inspect_scheduled_job">inspect_scheduled_job()</a>, <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.suspend_scheduled_job">suspend_scheduled_job()</a>, <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.resume_scheduled_job">resume_scheduled_job()</a>, <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.trigger_scheduled_job">trigger_scheduled_job()</a>, and <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.delete_scheduled_job">delete_scheduled_job()</a>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " class="language-python "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># List your active scheduled jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> list_scheduled_jobs
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>list_scheduled_jobs()

<span class="hljs-comment"># Only list scheduled jobs with the given labels</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>list_scheduled_jobs(labels={<span class="hljs-string">&quot;env&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;prod&quot;</span>})

<span class="hljs-comment"># Inspect the status of a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> inspect_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>inspect_scheduled_job(scheduled_job_id)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Suspend (pause) a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> suspend_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>suspend_scheduled_job(scheduled_job_id)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Resume a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> resume_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>resume_scheduled_job(scheduled_job_id)

<span class="hljs-comment"># Trigger a scheduled job to run right now (does not change the schedule)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> trigger_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job = trigger_scheduled_job(scheduled_job_id)
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>job.url

<span class="hljs-comment"># Delete a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span><span class="hljs-keyword">from</span> huggingface_hub <span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> delete_scheduled_job
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>delete_scheduled_job(scheduled_job_id)<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="trigger-jobs-with-webhooks" class="header-link webhooks</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Webhooks allow you to listen for new changes on specific repos or to all repos belonging to particular set of users/organizations (not just your repos, but any repo).</p> <p>Use <a href="/docs/huggingface_hub/v1.31.0.rc0/en/package_reference/hf_api#huggingface_hub.HfApi.create_webhook">create_webhook()</a> to create a webhook that triggers a Job when a <span class="hljs-comment"># Example: Creating a webhook that triggers a Job</span>
webhook = create_webhook(
    job_id=job_id,
    watched=[{<span class="hljs-string">&quot;type&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;user&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;name&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;your-username&quot;</span>}, {<span class="hljs-string">&quot;type&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;org&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;name&quot;</span>: <span class="hljs-string">&quot;your-org-name&quot;</span>}],
    domains=[<span class="hljs-string">&quot;repo&quot;</span>, <span class="hljs-string">&quot;discussion&quot;</span>],
    secret=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;your-secret&quot;</span>
)<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The webhook triggers the Job with the webhook payload in the environment variable <code>WEBHOOK_PAYLOAD</code>.
You can find more information on webhooks in the <a href="./webhooks">Webhooks documentation</a>.</p> <a class="!text-gray-400 !no-underline text-sm flex items-center not-prose mt-4" href="https://github.com/huggingface/huggingface_hub/blob/main/docs/source/en/guides/jobs.md" target="_blank"><svg class="mr-1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M31,16l-7,7l-1.41-1.41L28.17,16l-5.58-5.59L24,9l7,7z"></path><path d="M1,16l7-7l1.41,1.41L3.83,16l5.58,5.59L8,23l-7-7z"></path><path d="M12.419,25.484L17.639,6.552l1.932,0.518L14.351,26.002z"></path></svg><!----> <span><span class="underline">Update</span> on GitHub</span></a><!----> <p></p><!--]--><!----><!--]--><!--]--><!--]--> <!--[-1--><!--]--><!--]-->
